Posts for 2015-02

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Jan 31 2015 19:14:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

.
संविधान को आम नागरिको ने लागू किया है, तथा उनकी इच्छा ही सर्वोपरि है , किन्तु संविधान क्या कहता है, इसकी व्याख्या सुप्रीम कोर्ट का विशेषधिकार है । मायने यह कि, संविधान में कुछ भी लिखा हो, उसका मतलब क्या है यह सिर्फ सुप्रीम कोर्ट के न्यायधीश ही निर्धारित करते है ।
सभी कानूनों की व्याख्या का अधिकार भी सुप्रीम कोर्ट के पास है ।
.
सुप्रीम कोर्ट को पॉवर है, कि वह मिनट से पहले पीएम, सीएम को दोषी करार दे कर पद छोड़ने के लिए बाध्य कर सकता है, संसद द्वारा बनाए हुए कानूनों को खारिज कर के रद्दी में फेंक सकता है।
.
कोर्ट की आलोचना करना भी न्यायलय की अवमानना है, अत: कोई व्यक्ति यदि कोर्ट के फैसले की निंदा करे तो कोर्ट उसे सलाखों के पीछे भेज सकता है ।
.
सुप्रीम कोर्ट के न्यायधिशो को हटाने की प्रक्रिया पीएम, सीएम आम आदमी आदि किसी के पास नहीं है । वह इस वन के अगाध सिंह है ।
.
सुप्रीम कोर्ट के न्यायधीशो को सिर्फ महाभियोग से हटाया जा सकता है, जो कि बेहद जटिल तथा दुरूह प्रक्रिया है ।
.
निरंकुश न्यायधीश सुस्त और भ्रष्ट न्याय व्यवस्था को जन्म देते है, सुस्त न्याय व्यवस्था अपराधियों के हौसले को बढ़ावा देती है, दंड के अभाव में सारे क़ानून रद्दी साबित होते है और पूरी व्यवस्था चरमरा जाती है ।
.
समाधान

1. जूरी प्रथा : न्याय व्यवस्था को चुस्त और साफ़ सुथरा बनाने के लिए हमें जज प्रणाली की तरह जूरी प्रथा को लागू करना चाहिए ।
.
.
जूरी प्रथा में प्रत्येक मुकदमे की सुनवाई के लिए मतदाता सूची में से 12-15 नागरिको का रेंडमली चयन किया जाता है, तथा नागरिको की यह ज्यूरी ही मुकदमो की सुनवाई करके फैसला देती है ।

प्रत्येक मुकदमे के लिए अलग ज्यूरी होने से मुकदमो के फैसले 15-20 दिन में आ जाते है तथा जज-वकील गठजोड़ की संभावनाएं समाप्त हो जाती है ।

अमेरिका ने अपनी न्याय व्यवस्था को निष्पक्ष और त्वरित बनाने के लिए इसी प्रणाली का उपयोग किया है ।
.
2. प्रजा अधीन न्यायधीश : प्रजातंत्र में सर्वोच्च सत्ता प्रजा होती है अत: न्यायधीश भी प्रजा अधीन होने चाहिए, ताकि भ्रष्ट आचरण करने पर प्रजा मतदान द्वारा न्यायधिशो को नौकरी से निकाल सके ।
.
प्रजा अधीन राजा समूह ने न्यायपालिका को स्वच्छ बनाने के लिए जूरी प्रथा तथा न्यायधीशो को प्रजा अधीन बनाने के लिए कानूनी ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किये है, जिन्हें गेजेट में प्रकाशित करने से भारत में निष्पक्ष, चुस्त और स्वच्छ न्यायपालिका की स्थापना होगी ।
.
प्रस्तावित कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट यहाँ देखें :
tinyurl. com/jurysys
.
यदि आप इन कानूनों का समर्थन करते है, तो अपने सांसद को SMS द्वारा निर्देश भेजें कि, जूरी प्रथा के क़ानून को गेजेट में प्रकाशित किया जाए ।
.

पुनश्च : जबकि भारत की अदालतों में 3 करोड़ मुकदमे लंबित है, बीजेपी कोंग्रेस तथा आम आदमी पार्टी पूरी ईमानदारी के साथ सम्मिलित रूप से जूरी प्रथा तथा प्रजा अधीन न्यायधीश के कानूनों का विरोध कर रहे है । स्पष्ट है कि उनकी रुचि न्यायिक सुधारों में नही है ।
.
नोट : SMS प्रारूप के लिए कमेन्ट बॉक्स देखें ।
.

________________

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Feb 01 2015 03:01:26 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why in THIS specific delhi election , NOTA is best long term choice , and only slightly poor short term choice
.
NOTA is loud cry for help, while voting for Congress , BJP or AAP is open willing submission to their pusedo-giri or arrogance and their paidmedia coverage
.
Say you given following choice "do you want to get robbed by and then enslaved by A , B or C ? And if you dont answer, then someone else will decide who will rob / enslave you". You might be tempted to pick the least bad robber-master. But if you say "NONE OF THESE ROBBER-MASTERS", then a person outside may hear your cry and will be motivated to work for your salvage. However, if you say "A , B or C" then that 4th person will think "well, you are happy with slavery-robbery of A, B or C, so why bother?" .
.
Now lets see delhi election. Congress / BJP are both openly pro-corruption and also oppose the law-drafts to improve education for the poor. etc. AAP is just pseido-anti-corruption and pseudo in everything.
.
So NOTA will be a clear message to activists at large that the voter hates all 3. While a vote for Congress / BJP will mean "I dont mind corruption" and vote for AAP will be "I do like his psuedo-anti-corruption stand",
.
So in THIS specific delhi election, pls campaign for NOTA , or any good independent
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Feb 01 2015 06:08:43 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

ArKe/AAP-2015-manifesto vs NaMo's speeches before LS elections ---- NaMo promised moon and mars in vague grey language ; ArKe is promising sun and stars in clear black and white language !! SOLUTION I propose --- vote for NOTA , and campaign for good law-drafts such as RTR / Jury etc
.
We have an open race of "who can lie more ; and who can lie more effectively" . . No wonder why PAID MEDIA WILL WIN ELECTIONS
.
One requires a lot of courage (or shamelessness ?) to say "every poor Indian family will get Rs 15 lakh" . And his andhbhagats will defend him by equally vague defences that "see, only said that there is black money per poor family ; he never said that every family will get that much money" OR "he never promised that money will come in 250 days" etc etc. But nevertheless it was a vague promise.
.
Same way, NaMo made many statement which will appear as promise but when one reads the speeches' transcripts, one can see that there were no promises made in clear language. eg ending cow slaughter, ending pink revolution, ending meat export, swadeshi, releasing temples from govt control , expelling bangladeshi, and even ending corruption !! Most voters after listening to his cleverly worded speeches were thinking that NaMo is promising all. Where as raw reading of the text transcripts shows that he had promised nothing. All in all, it was promising moon and mars in vague grey language.
.
But AAP Arke are going ahead. In clear back and white language, they are making promises that cant be met even if you multiply Delhi's total revenues and total central govt grants by 10 times !!! New colleges, new stadiums, water pipelines, new electricity generation plants, new solar plants, massive improvement in transmission and distribution of electricity, halving electricity rate, free water for 20000 liter per month, 10000s of CCTV camera and staff to repair and monitor them , improving transportation and what not. Almost every wish of every activist born in past 5000 years is there !! And not only it is there in manifesto --- it given in clear language in black and white !! One needs a lot of courage (or shamelessness ?) to make such brash promises !!!
.
Has AAP or anyone made an estimate of costs of AAP's all promises? I havent. But a cursory glance makes me feel that it will be at least 10 times more than Delhi's annual revenue of Rs 40000 crore
.
Speeches of BJP leaders and AAP manifesto looks we have some race on who can speak bigger lies, who can make taller promises and who can make a bigger fool out of voters. The only difference is that BJP leaders' are more seasoned and so their lies are all in grey / vague language. While AAP is speaking lies in black and white and clear language.
.
SOLUTION?
.
The short term solution is to vote for NOTA --- tall all three that we dont believe in you. The long term solution is that activists should stop wasting time after Congress , BJP and AAP and instead publicize good law-drafts such as Right to recall, Jury System etc etc
.
Please send following order to your MP via SMS --- " Dear MP , please print Right to Recall specific Minister law-draft in the Gazette so that we voters can replace Ministers in charge of dept , other than Defence , Home etc and dept such as Education, Chemical, Fertilizer etc . For full draft , pls see http://tinyurl.com/RtrMinister "

Read full post →

Why I ask people to read DRAFTS and not give much emphasis on videos
,
Experiments after experiments show that those who read maths/science learn maths/science. while those who watch video showed no significant improvement !!
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Feb 01 2015 13:34:27 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

On 28-nov-2010 , two days before Rajiv Dixitjee was murdered , he had outlined the plan he had proposed. His proposal was to fielld candidates in almost all 543 seats , and he was hopeful to get 400 winners. YES, 400 WINNERS. And the goal was to get Right to Recall passed. All this may have happened, but too bad, he was murdered 2 days later

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Feb 01 2015 15:22:03 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why vote for NOTA in this specific delhi election?
.
Where will ArKe get money for all promises he made in manifesto?
.
Simple ---- NaMo will soon bring black money back, by which every family will get Rs 15 lakh !!! And ArKe will impose ONLY 10% tax on it. That should be enough to fulfil all promises ArKe is making !!!
.

.

http://www.ndtv.com/assembly/if-my-luck-can-benefit-the-country-why-elect-someone-unlucky-says-pm-modi-736208
.
'If my Luck can Benefit the Country, Why Elect Someone Unlucky,' Says PM Modi
.
NEW DELHI, INDIA: In his second rally ahead of the Delhi Assembly elections, Prime Minister Narendra Modi tore into the opposition Congress and the Aam Aadmi Party, alleging the two parties were competing to tell lies and were in league when it came to conspiring behind the scenes. "They wake up in the morning and think of what lies to propagate. Never before has any Delhi election seen such a heavy dependence on falsehoods," he said at the rally in West Delhi's Dwarka.

On stage with PM Modi were the party's Delhi unit chief Satish Upadhyay, senior party leaders and the 12 candidates of the region from the BJP and ally Akali Dal. The Prime Minister was welcomed by Kiran Bedi - his choice for the top post should the BJP win. In a reference to her past as a senior police officer, the Prime Minister said he could leave the city's traffic troubles to her.

His concern, he said, was that every family should have a home by the 75th year of India's Independence. The year that was wasted was a setback by 25 years, he said. Asking for votes in the name of good governance and development, he said the opposition was attributing the fall in petrol price to his luck. "If my luck can benefit the country, why do you want to elect someone unlucky," he said to huge cheers from the audience. A chunk of his speech was devoted to the track record of the Union government -- the successful implementation of projects like the Jan Dhan Yojana and the country's standing with key foreign powers.

"No matter which world leader stands in front on me... I can look them in the eye and talk as an equal," PM Modi said, in an indirect reference to the recent visit by US President Barack Obama. For Delhi, electing the BJP, he hinted, would also important since the legislators will be directly under his control. "Won't they be afraid of Modi? Won't they think many times over before slacking in their work or doing anything wrong?" As for the AAP, he said, "They will only be interested in grabbing airtime on television. They won't have time for people."

Wed Feb 04 2015 10:36:37 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. Rs 2 crore donation to AAP --- pointless issue. The donations came via cheque . So donation acceptor's irresponsibles are morally as well as legally minimal. Also, inland cheque transactions i.e. cheque transactions where both parties are in India , can never create money laundering. In any case, the Income Tax dept and Election Commission can obtain cheque number, bank name etc and establish any case if there is.
.
===================
.
2. SoMoKe are supporting Mumbai High Court's decision that will effectively ensure that foreign companies dont have to pay any capital gains tax !! How? Say company X is registered in India. So when A sells share of X to B, A has to pay capital gains tax. But lets say a foreign company Y owns share of X. And A and B are foreign companies. Then A sells Y to B, and capital gains tax will be zero even though effective ownership of X did change !!! This is contrary to nexus rule aka section-9 of Indian Income Tax act . But SoMoKe have supported this High Court ruling.
.
Solution I propose : if shares are owned by a foreign entity, then every 5 year , a tax should be applies on it which should be 30% of increase in its value i.e. all share owned by foreign entities should be subject to deemed capital gains tax.
.
Otherwise, as per SoMoKe's stand, the defactor rule will be "gora shall pay no capital gains taxes ; desi must pay capital gains taxes" !! SoMoke andbhagats are also supporting this defacto stand.
.
=====
.
3 RSS-apex and RSS-workers support Maharashtra Govt 's decision for field trial of GM crop. And so do SoMoKe and SoMoKe-andbhagats. Only we at Right to recall group are opposing GM crops and BT crops. BST-workers too are shouting slogans supporting MH-govt. So please leave CoBhApSwaRa-workers and pls work with Right to Recall group
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Feb 04 2015 20:32:47 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

क्या किरण बेदी को साक्षात्कार देने से परहेज है ?
.

लोकसभा चुनावों में मतदान के दौरान पेड मिडिया ने लगभग सभी चेनल्स पर मोदी के कई साक्षात्कारो का अनवरत प्रसारण करके बीजेपी को निर्णायक बढ़त दिला दी थी, अब वही घटनाक्रम जब केजरीवाल के साथ दोहराया जा रहा है तो पेड मिडिया मोदी साहेब की आँखों में चुभ रहा है ।
.

ऐसे कांटे के मुकाबले में जीत का फैसला या तो EVM करेगी या पेड मिडिया, जो कि MNCs और मिशनरीज़ के नियंत्रण में है ।
.

सभी ओपिनियन पोल पेड होते है, तथा उसे ही जीतता हुआ दिखाते है, जिन्हें सर्वे करने वाले जिताना चाहते है ।
.

गफलत : पूर्वोत्तर के नागरिको को प्रवासी दिखाने के केजरीवाल के एजेंडे को बीजेपी ने 'गलती' से हाइजेक कर लिया है ।
.

कुछ बिंदु जिन पर बीजेपी, आम आदमी पार्टी और कोंग्रेस तीनो पार्टियाँ सहमत है :

1. तीनो पार्टियां सहमत है कि राजनेतिक दलों को RTI के दायरे में नही आना चाहिए, किन्तु तीनो पार्टियां चंदे की प्रक्रिया को 'पारदर्शी' बनाना चाहते है ।

2. तीनो पार्टियाँ झुग्गियों को नियमित करना चाहती है, बिजली पानी सस्ता करना चाहती है, सभी को पक्के घर देना चाहती है, तथा महिलाओ को सुरक्षा देना चाहती है ।
तीनो पार्टियाँ इस पर सहमत है कि ये सभी कार्य किन कानूनों को पास करके किये जायेंगे उन कानूनों की पड़ताल सत्ता में आने के बाद की जायेगी ।

3. तीनो पार्टियाँ भ्रष्टाचार ख़त्म करने के लिए आवश्यक राईट टू रिकाल कानूनों का विरोध करने पर सहमत है ।

4. तीनो पार्टियां सहमत है कि जनता के पास सिर्फ चुनने का अधिकार होना चाहिए, हटाने का नहीं ।
.
.

यदि MNCs केजरीवाल को जिताने के लिए EVM हेक नहीं कर पाती है, तो केजरीवाल EVM के सिर पर हार का ठीकरा फोड़ सकते है ।
पृष्ठभूमि निर्माण के लिए केजरीवाल ने EVM में गड़बड़ी की आशंका व्यक्त की है, जिसके सम्बन्ध में चुनाव आयोग ने उन्हें किसी भी प्रकार की धांधली न होने देने के लिए मुतमईन किया है ।
.
केजरीवाल चुनाव आयोग के जवाब से मुतमईन भी हो गए है, मतलब बहल गए है ।
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Feb 05 2015 04:13:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How vegetarian is LESS violent than non-vegetarian food.
.
My take on veg vs nonveg food is --- each one on his own. My proposals are to imprison those who slaughter cows, but for non-cows, slaughter houses will be legal. And economic laws I have proposed will reduce subsidies that nonveg food is getting and so nonveg will become more expensive that it is now, without any tax. So nonveg food consumption will decrease. Thats about it.
.
But one issue that comes is that nonveg food is violence. And the nonvegitarians often make counter-argument --- that plants also have life and also vegism is also violent. This post is to refute this SPECIFIC counter-argument only.
.
1. To produce 1 kg of meat, some 10 kg to 20 kg wheat equivalent grains/pulse/vegetables are needed. So 1 kg of meat will involve MORE violence than 1 kg of grain/pulse/vegetable. So even if killing plan is violent, then also killing animals is 10-20-more times MORE VIOLENT.
.
2. The plants have lesser nuerons and have only name sake nervous system. (please google on nervous system in plants). The nervous system is only to guide the plant to make its limited movement towards sunlight , water etc. Experiments show that plants make attempt to move towards sunlight, move towards water, move away from toxins, but plants make no attempt to move away from grazing animals or even fruit plucking animals or insects. IOW, plants consider getting eaten as their destiny, Whereas animals make explicit attempts to move away from grazers. IOW, plants perhaps consider being eaten as their destiny.
.
3. PAIN AND VIOLENCE CAUSE BY ASSOCIATION : The plants have no association, while animals have. eg when a human is killed, the OTHER humans around him feel pain. Same way, when an animal is killed, the other animals around him feel fear, pain and also miss him. When one plant is taken away from field, the other plants in field feel no pains. At least, thats what experiments show. May be , future experiments will show association between plants. But so far , there is none.
.
4. Plants have no parent-child emotional relation . eg in almost all mammals , parents sacrifice for their kids, Where as in plants , when a seed falls and tries to become plant, the parent plant will actually end up killing its growth by absorbing away all sunlight, water and minerals !!! IOW, there is no known emotional bonding amongst plants. So killing one plant doesnt increase fear, pain etc in other plants. So killing plants is LESS violent than animals.
.
5. DISPERSAL OF SEEDS---- plants cant survive without dispersal of seeds (pls google on dispersal of seeds). And for dispersal, they depend on animals. And so food the incentive plants offer to animals for dispersal. I am NOT joking or kidding. Please google on dispersal of seeds and you will see that animals play important role for plants by eating plants !!
.
If every fruit of of a tree falls below and doesnt get moved elsewhere, then that fruit will simply dont become a tree because the existing tree will keep soaking water / minerals etc. So seeds need dispersal. And thats the job of animals --- they disperse the seeds by eating plants / fruits. So plants benefit from animals' eating of plants. But when we eat animals, the animals dont get anything in return.
.
So in all cases , nonvegeism is more violent than vegism.
.
=======================
.
All in all, this agruments will make zero difference. Those who eat nonveg rightly or wrongly believe that nonveg food will give them some advantages. And all these arguments will NOT convince them to make them give up their advantage.
.
Many jeev-daya-vaadies believe that showing slaughter video will make any difference. IMO, they are all wasting time. It will effect only at most 5% meat eaters and then remaining 95% will become immune to slaughter videos. If slaughter video were to make difference, then animal sacrifices would have never started.
.
If jeevdaya-vaadies are sincere, then they should promote law-drafts which end WRONGFUL subsidies and cross-subsidies to non-veg food. They should promote law-draft which require show-makers to mark ;labels like "made from leather of animal which died natural death and meat was NOT used for food" or opposite mark as the case may be. And they should promote law-drafts that would force milk makers to mark milk as "milk giving animal will NOT be slaughtered" or have mark "milk giving animal may be slaughtered" as may be the case. In such case, that shoe and that milk will become more expensive but this will also make meat more expensive !!! And that will reduce meat consumption.
.
As a simple measure --- nonveg consumption across world and across India is rising and irisng and rising. And in India, it is rising across all communities irrespective of religions.
.
So all in all , slaughter videos and arguments like "plant eating is less violent" are like water on stone. After 1000 years of water fall, the stone will lose 1% of if weight. Instead focus on law-drafts that can reduce slaughters.
The law-drafts are in Hindi https://fb.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152908801793103/ and in english at https://fb.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152747542153103/ .
.
Pls download them and work on these law-drafts, if you want to save cows and animals.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Feb 05 2015 04:13:46 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How vegetarian is LESS violent than non-vegetarian food.
.
My take on veg vs nonveg food is --- each one on his own. My proposals are to imprison those who slaughter cows, but for non-cows, slaughter houses will be legal. And economic laws I have proposed will reduce subsidies that nonveg food is getting and so nonveg will become more expensive that it is now, without any tax. So nonveg food consumption will decrease. Thats about it.
.
But one issue that comes is that nonveg food is violence. And the nonvegitarians often make counter-argument --- that plants also have life and also vegism is also violent. This post is to refute this SPECIFIC counter-argument only.
.
1. To produce 1 kg of meat, some 10 kg to 20 kg wheat equivalent grains/pulse/vegetables are needed. So 1 kg of meat will involve MORE violence than 1 kg of grain/pulse/vegetable. So even if killing plan is violent, then also killing animals is 10-20-more times MORE VIOLENT.
.
2. The plants have lesser nuerons and have only name sake nervous system. (please google on nervous system in plants). The nervous system is only to guide the plant to make its limited movement towards sunlight , water etc. Experiments show that plants make attempt to move towards sunlight, move towards water, move away from toxins, but plants make no attempt to move away from grazing animals or even fruit plucking animals or insects. IOW, plants consider getting eaten as their destiny, Whereas animals make explicit attempts to move away from grazers. IOW, plants perhaps consider being eaten as their destiny.
.
3. PAIN AND VIOLENCE CAUSE BY ASSOCIATION : The plants have no association, while animals have. eg when a human is killed, the OTHER humans around him feel pain. Same way, when an animal is killed, the other animals around him feel fear, pain and also miss him. When one plant is taken away from field, the other plants in field feel no pains. At least, thats what experiments show. May be , future experiments will show association between plants. But so far , there is none.
.
4. Plants have no parent-child emotional relation . eg in almost all mammals , parents sacrifice for their kids, Where as in plants , when a seed falls and tries to become plant, the parent plant will actually end up killing its growth by absorbing away all sunlight, water and minerals !!! IOW, there is no known emotional bonding amongst plants. So killing one plant doesnt increase fear, pain etc in other plants. So killing plants is LESS violent than animals.
.
5. DISPERSAL OF SEEDS---- plants cant survive without dispersal of seeds (pls google on dispersal of seeds). And for dispersal, they depend on animals. And so food the incentive plants offer to animals for dispersal. I am NOT joking or kidding. Please google on dispersal of seeds and you will see that animals play important role for plants by eating plants !!
.
If every fruit of of a tree falls below and doesnt get moved elsewhere, then that fruit will simply dont become a tree because the existing tree will keep soaking water / minerals etc. So seeds need dispersal. And thats the job of animals --- they disperse the seeds by eating plants / fruits. So plants benefit from animals' eating of plants. But when we eat animals, the animals dont get anything in return.
.
So in all cases , nonvegeism is more violent than vegism.
.
=======================
.
All in all, this agruments will make zero difference. Those who eat nonveg rightly or wrongly believe that nonveg food will give them some advantages. And all these arguments will NOT convince them to make them give up their advantage.
.
Many jeev-daya-vaadies believe that showing slaughter video will make any difference. IMO, they are all wasting time. It will effect only at most 5% meat eaters and then remaining 95% will become immune to slaughter videos. If slaughter video were to make difference, then animal sacrifices would have never started.

And they should promote law-drafts that would force milk makers to mark milk as "milk giving animal will NOT be slaughtered" or have mark "milk giving animal may be slaughtered" as may be the case. In such case, that shoe and that milk will become more expensive but this will also make meat more expensive !!! And that will reduce meat consumption.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Thu Feb 05 2015 12:26:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

1. Should movie-makers be allowed to charge different ticket rate from different theatres?
.
The medicine manufactures and most manufacturers are NOT allowed to charge different rates from different buyers. They can offer quantity discounts , but cant have structure that "medicine in delhi will be sold at Rs 200 and same medicine in ahmedabad will be sold at Rs 100 !!". . So should movie makers be required to take same price from all theateres?
.
my proposal : movie manufacturer must be required to collect same price from all theaters. He may decrease the price of the movie after a few days (eg he can ask for Rs 200 on first day and decrease / increase price later). But his all India price should be same across India.
.
=======
.
2. Can a movie maker be allowed to block viewing of its movies in a PARTICULAR cinema?
.
FMCG makers and medicine makers, and most manufacturers cannot stop shop from selling their product. eg a soap maker cannot say that "xyz shop cant sell my product" unless it confines to its own outlets. So should movie makers be required to allow any theater owner who pays its designated price to show the movie?
.
My proposal : movie makers should NOT be allowed to stop a theatre from showing the movie as long as theater is paying the rate movie manufacturer is asking (and movie manufactuer must ask same rate from all theaters).
.
You may post your propsals
.
These laws are important to break the neck of movie industry which has been running amok and reckless.

The Delhi feb-2015 election shows that MNC-paidmedia is now far more powerful than BJP + RSS + VHP + BST + all its leaders + all its workers. The reason is --- andhbhakti of workers
.
Solution I propose : the nationalist should stop wasting time after CoBhAp-leaders and CoBhApSwaRa-workers and focus on law-drafts that can reduce REAL problems such as rising dominance of FDI = MNC-owners, rising conversions, rising terrorism , demographic mess, corruption, weakening manufacturing of India etc etc
.
The MNC-owners had proved in dec-2013 Delhi elections that using their paidmedia they can swing 15% or more national votes in favor of ArKe. And thus they had proved that they can ensure that NaMo cant become PM. This forced NaMo to accept all conditions laid out by MNC-owners such as FDI in railways, defence and everything. NaMo also accepted GM food and agreed to delay release of census-2011 numbers.
.
So MNC-owners in feb-2014 ordered their paidmedia to pull plugs on ArKe. So by 1-feb-2014 , paidmedia was all gaga over NaMo , and NaMo won by a landslide.
.
Now come feb-2015 , and paidmedia sided with ArKe , And we see that BJP / NaMo are in serious mess.
.
The nationalists refused to campaign for law-drafts to reduce corruption, reduce harassment factory owners face, reduce poverty, reduce flat prices, expel bangladeshi etc. And so MNC-owners got oppurtunity to plant a pusedo-solution such as ArKe as real solution,
.
In 230 days, NaMo couldnt / didnt convert Doordarshan into a true national media that would expose foreign agents like ArKe. Also, NaMo couldnt / didnt reduce problems like corruption in police, corruption in courts etc. And so foreign agents like Arke are now having a field day.
.
Even if BJP wins, the fact that ArKe will get over 35% votes speaks volumes. Next , MNC-owners using paidmedia will make sure that ArKe advances in all states across India and we will have
.
SOLUTION : solution I propose is that nationalists should spread information on good law-drafts that can reduce the problems, such as TCP , Right to Recall , JurySys , DDMRCAM etc, and they should stop wasting time in SoMoKe-andhbhakti. I have given my proposed drafts in fb.com/notes/10151308223491922
.

.
थोड़े दिन और रुको .
धारा 370 तो जुमला था - राजनाथ सिंह।
पिंक रेवोलुशन भी जुमला था - नमो।
बांग्लादेशी मुदा भी चुनावी नारा था - अरुण जेटली।
स्वदेशी का तो नारा ही चुनाव के लिए होता है - बाबा रामदेव
राम मंदिर तो हमरा जुमला है - आरएसएस।
सामान नागरिक संहिता ??? यह क्या होता है - उमा भारती।
.
सरकार भी जुमलों जुमलों में बन गयी न। कार्यकर्ता अब तो समझ जाये एक इंसान देश नहीं बदल सकता। स्थतिया बदलने के लिए हमे कानूनी प्रक्रियाओ की जरुरत है। देश को राइट टू रिकॉल , जूरी सिस्टम , टी सी पी , डी डी आर सी एम , आदि कानूनी की जरुरत है न की पेड मीडिया पर दिखने वाले सुपरमैन नेताओ की।
.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Feb 06 2015 09:42:34 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Vinod Prajapat:

दिल्ली 2014 के चुनावो ने यह बता दिया है की बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो द्वारा पोषित मीडिया अब नमो, भाजपा, आरएसएस , विहिप और उनके कार्यकर्ताओ से कही अधिक शक्तिशाली है। और उसका कारन है कार्यकर्ताओ की अंधभक्ति।
.
इसका समाधान यह है की कार्यकर्ता भाजपा /कांग्रेस /आप /आरएसएस / आदि संगठनो के पीछे समय बर्बाद न करे और कुछ जरुरी कानूनी प्रक्रियाओ का समर्थन करे जो Bharat की समस्याएं जैसे की FDI , आतंकवाद , धर्म परिवर्तन , भ्रस्टाचार , कमजोर उद्योग , कमजोर सेना , गो हत्या , धरा 370 , बांग्लादेशी मुद्दा आदि समस्याओं का समाधान हो सके।
.
.
बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो के मालिको ने दिसंबर 2013 में यह भी भी दिखा दिया था की कैसे पेड मीडिया का उपयोग करके वे कैसे 15 % या उसके अधिक वोट केजरीवाल की तरफ मोड़ सकते है। और नमो को कैसे प्रधानमंत्री बनाने से रोक सकते है। इसी कारन नमो को उनकी सारी मांगे जैसे की रेलवे , रक्षा आदि क्षेत्रो में FDI माननी पड़ी। यहाँ तक ज़ी एम फ़ूड की मांग भी स्वीकार कर ली।
.
इसीलिए पेड मीडिया ने 1 feb से नमो राग गाना चालू कर दिया और एक बड़ी जीत मिली। अब feb 2015 में पेड मीडिया केजरीवाल की तरफ है और नमो और भाजपा परेशानी में दिख रहे है।
.
.
राष्ट्रवादियो ने ऐसे किसी कानून की मांग नमो से नहीं करी बल्कि अंधभक्ति में लीं हो गए इसी कारन बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो को एक नकली हीरो अरविन्द केजरीवाल बिठाने का मौका मिल गया। नमो ने 9 महीनो में दूरदर्शन को एक सच्चे राष्ट्रीय चैनल नहीं बनाया।
.
अब भाजपा भले ही जीत जाये लेकिन केजरीवाल को ३५ % से ज्यादा वोट मिलना तय है और आने वाले चुनावो में राज्यवार आम आदमी पार्टी पैर पसरेगी। और पेड मीडिया उनकी जीत को सुनिचित करेगा
.
समाधान : - रहट्रवादी कार्यकर्ता कानूनी ड्राफ्ट जैसे राइट टू रिकॉल , टी सी पी , जूरी सिस्टम , आदि का प्रचार करे तथा पार्टियो और नेताओ की अंधभक्ति न करे .fb.com/notes/10151308223491922 में सरे प्रस्तावित ड्राफ्ट है।

बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो के मालिको ने दिसंबर 2013 में यह भी भी दिखा दिया था की कैसे पेड मीडिया का उपयोग करके वे कैसे 15 % या उसके अधिक वोट केजरीवाल की तरफ मोड़ सकते है। और नमो को कैसे प्रधानमंत्री बनाने से रोक सकते है। इसी कारन नमो को उनकी सारी मांगे जैसे की रेलवे , रक्षा आदि क्षेत्रो में FDI माननी पड़ी। यहाँ तक ज़ी एम फ़ूड की मांग भी स्वीकार कर ली।
.
इसीलिए पेड मीडिया ने 1 feb से नमो राग गाना चालू कर दिया और एक बड़ी जीत मिली। अब feb 2015 में पेड मीडिया केजरीवाल की तरफ है और नमो और भाजपा परेशानी में दिख रहे है।
.
.
राष्ट्रवादियो ने ऐसे किसी कानून की मांग नमो से नहीं करी बल्कि अंधभक्ति में लीं हो गए इसी कारन बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो को एक नकली हीरो अरविन्द केजरीवाल बिठाने का मौका मिल गया। नमो ने 9 महीनो में दूरदर्शन को एक सच्चे राष्ट्रीय चैनल नहीं बनाया।
.
अब भाजपा भले ही जीत जाये लेकिन केजरीवाल को ३५ % से ज्यादा वोट मिलना तय है और आने वाले चुनावो में राज्यवार आम आदमी पार्टी पैर पसरेगी। और पेड मीडिया उनकी जीत को सुनिचित करेगा
.
समाधान : - रहट्रवादी कार्यकर्ता कानूनी ड्राफ्ट जैसे राइट टू रिकॉल , टी सी पी , जूरी सिस्टम , आदि का प्रचार करे तथा पार्टियो और नेताओ की अंधभक्ति न करे .fb.com/notes/10151308223491922 में सरे प्रस्तावित ड्राफ्ट है।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Feb 06 2015 16:09:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The most important function of the society is to manufacture weapons.
.
And the second most important function of society is to manufacture medicine (and the word "medicine" includes medicinal equipment). And third comes all gizmo from watch to telephone to movie projector to car to airplanes.
.
A society which doesnt manufacture weapons will soon get robbed and enslaved. And a society which doesnt manufacture medicine and gizmos will end up importing, importing and importing to an extent that it will become so poor that there will be nothing left to rob !! And in the last stage of extreme poverty or debt, such "import everything manufacture nothing" nation will not have money to manufacture weapons and then it will get enslaved.
.
Some 80 years ago, these 3 facts were triviality. The importance of weapons was known to all and accepted by all. They were know to even most illiterate and accepted by all. But today, in India, when I say that "weapon manufacturing is most important", most people find this statement repulsive. They think I am some uncivilized irrational paranoid unenlighted caveman. And they seem to believe that a modern , enlightened, rational etc man is supposed to think that "weapons are now useless" !!! May be I am uncivilized. Or may be, these people around me are all living in some fools' paradise. They consider my "weapon making is most important" statement as something that will derail the journey of India towards a paradise of peace. And I think that their thoughts will make us so weaponless that it may be too too late to save what is left of India.
.
If weapons are not important, then my stand can be rubbished. But if weapon making is MOST important, then a question comes ---- how comes huge % of population is unaware that "weapon manufacturing is most important" . And why are they equally unaware that
.
(a) Chinese Military has 10 times more weapons than Indian Military,
.
(b) American Military has 100 times more weapons than Indian Military,
.
(c) weapons owned by civilians of Pakistan are 10 times weapons owned by civilians of India despite India's population being 6 times.
.
(d) Pakistan makes better guns than India !! (google on Khyber pass copy)
.
(e) India and Pakistan both had lost Kargil war, and it was USA which had won Kargil war !!!
.
If we recallists are some psycotic fools, then all is well. But if we are right, and activists / citizens at large are unaware, then what can be the reason for such massive unawareness?
.
AFAIS the reason is ---- the paidmediamen and the paid-textbook-authors are paid by foreigners to hide these simple facts , including hiding the fact that weapon-manufacturing is most important function of the society"
.
And sadly, the activists \ citizens read nothing but textbooks and paidmediamen's paid-newspapers and paid-Tv-channels.
.
We are in a deep mess. And psuedo-solutions like FDI , NaMo-bhakti, ArKe-bhakti, Sonia-bhakti etc are only making matters worse.
.
Solution I propose is that activists should publicize law-drafts like Right to recall doordarshan chairman , so that doordarshan becomes free from leaders who are chained by west.
.
If you support Right to Recall over Doordarshan Chairma, pls send following order to MP via SMS
.
"Dear MP, please print Right to Recall Doordarshan Chairman draft in Gazette , to reduce the influence of paidmedia in India. For full draft, please see http://fb.com/mehtarahulc/posts/10152299911721922 "

.
So ArKe will get over 40 seats.
.

Because ArKe heavily depends on MNC-paid paidmedia and MNC-owners oppose law-drafts needed to improve internal economy. So ArKe will do nothing to fixi local manufacturing, If Arke tries to bring law-drafts to fixi local manufacturing, then paidmedia can unseat him within days.
.
So finally, ArKe's ability to deliver will depend on FDI and FDI only.
.
And same is now true with NaMo . The experience of past 230 days has proved that NaMo cant pass any law that will reduce inefficiences of factory owners . Thats why our REAL manufacturing has been shrinking and shrinking, and import from China is rising and rising.
.
So essentially, success of ArKe / NaMo and all leaders now depend on FDI-bringers namely MNC-owners.
.
All in all, we have 3 leaders --- SoGa , NaMo, Arke, whom we think are "competing to serve us commons". But reality is --- they will soon compete for FDI and FDI only !!!
.
And FDI will bring huge temporary prosperity. Jobs will rise, income will rise, "manufacturing" by foreign company inside India will rise creating illusion that "made in India" is rising, imports are decreasing. And all will be fine till repatriation crisis hit from the sky --- but that will be at least 10-20 years from now.
.
Solution?
.
Solution I propose is that activists should study what is repatriation crisis, gather numbers of debt plus FDI , which gives total potential repatraition burden, and explain it to fellow activists. And we should order MPs via SMS to print law-draft that repatraition demanded by a company must be below the export income it has earned. And we should explain to all activists, that SoMoKe are opposing "repatriation by exports only" condition , and thus expose all three.
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Feb 08 2015 05:18:29 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

B5. प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश(FDI) पुनर्भरण के लिए राजपत्र अधिसूचना ( प्रस्तावित )

भारतीय नागरिको,

जब कोई विदेशी कंपनी भारत में निवेश करती है तो उसे व्यवसाय के लिए जमाएं, अंश, भूमि, श्रम आदि की आवश्यकता होती है ।
इन गतिविधियों को करने के लिए विदेशी कंपनी को भारतीय मुद्रा की आवश्यकता होती है, अत: अमुक विदेशी कम्पनी रिज़र्व बेंक ऑफ़ इण्डिया को डॉलर जमा करके उसी अनुपात में भारतीय मुद्रा अर्थात रुपया प्राप्त करती है ।
.

रिज़र्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया दो शर्तो के साथ डॉलर जमा करके रूपये का भुगतान करता है :

1. पुनर्भर्णिय : इस व्यवस्था के तहत व्यक्ति रिजर्व बैन्क ऑफ़ इण्डिया में रूपये जमा करके फिर से डॉलर की मांग कर सकता है ।
2. अपुनर्भरणीय : इस व्यवस्था में व्यक्ति रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ़ इण्डिया में रूपये जमा करके डॉलर की मांग नहीं कर सकता है ।
.

मौजूदा समय में संस्थागत विदेशी निवेश या प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश के तहत निवेश करने वाली कम्पनियों को उनके द्वारा अर्जित किये गए पूंजीगत तथा अन्य लाभों पर पुन:भरण का विकल्प दिया गया है । किन्तु ऐसी स्थिति में जबकि हमारे पास डॉलर या विदेशी मुद्रा का पर्याप्त कोष नहीं है, विदेशी मुद्रा की यह कमी हमारे लिए कभी भी गंभीर विदेशी मुद्रा संकट का कारण बन जायेगी तथा हम विदेशी मुद्रा के क़र्ज़ में लगातार डूबते जायेंगे ।
.

इसीलिए यह आवश्यक है कि प्रत्यक्ष विदेशी निवेश के जरिये आने वाले निवेश को अपुन:भरणीय नियम के अंतर्गत ही स्वीकार किया जाए । अत: रिज़र्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया या भारत सरकार को विदेशी कम्पनियों द्वारा अर्जित किये गए मुनाफे के बदले में डॉलर के भुगतान का कोई वादा नहीं करना चाहिए, बल्कि यह निर्धारित करना चाहिए कि अमुक कम्पनियां डॉलर खुले बाजार से ख़रीदे ।
.

यह व्यवस्था लागू करने से भारतीय अर्थव्यवस्था किसी भी समय खड़े हो सकने वाले इस गंभीर संकट से बच जायेगी ।
.

इसके अलावा निजी व्यष्टि को रिज़र्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया की अनुमति से विदेशी बेंको के खाते में डॉलर या विदेशी मुद्रा रखने की छूट दी जानी चाहिए । निर्यात से अर्जित विदेशी मुद्रा को वे विदेशी बेंको के खाते में रख सकेंगे, तथा उस आय पर उन्हें आयकर की सामान्य दरो के अनुसार कर देना होगा ।

======

ड्राफ्ट का प्रारम्भ :

.

[1]. जिलाधीश के लिए निर्देश :
यदि कोई नागरिक मतदाता इस क़ानून में संशोधन चाहता है, तो वह वांछित संशोधन के लिए एक शपथपत्र जिलाधीश कार्यालय में दे सकेगा । इस शपथपत्र को जिलाधीश या उसका सहायक प्रति पृष्ठ 20 रू की दर से शुल्क लेकर प्राधानमंत्री की वेबसाईट पर डाल देगा ।
.

[2]. पटवारी/तलाटी के लिए निर्देश :
यदि कोई नागरिक मतदाता ऐसे शपथपत्र पर या उसमें वर्णित किसी धारा में बदलाव के लिए हां/ना दर्ज कराता है, तो पटवारी ऐसी राय को 3 रू लेकर दर्ज करेगा तथा इसे प्रधानमन्त्री की वेबसाईट पर डाल देगा ।
.

[3]. हाँ या ना की संख्या के आधार पर प्रधानमन्त्री/मुख्यमंत्री फैसला लेने के लिए बाध्य नही होंगे ।
.

[4]. परिभाषाएं

इस अधिनियम में 'कंपनी' शब्द किसी भी भारतीय नागरिक या भारतीय कंपनी या कोई भारतीय गैर सरकारी संस्था जैसे ट्रस्ट आदि या कोई विदेशी नागरिक या विदेशी कंपनी या विदेशी सरकार या विदेशी गैर सरकारी संस्था जैसे कि कोई विदेशी ट्रस्ट आदि के लिए प्रयुक्त किया गया है ।

इस अधिनियम में 'भारतीय कंपनी' शब्द किसी भी भारतीय नागरिक या भारतीय कंपनी या भारतीय गैर सरकारी संस्था जैसे ट्रस्ट आदि के लिए प्रयुक्त किया गया है ।

इस अधिनियम में 'विदेशी कंपनी' शब्द किसी भी विदेशी नागरिक या विदेशी कंपनी या विदेशी सरकार या विदेशी गैर सरकारी संस्था जैसे कोई ट्रस्ट आदि के लिए प्रयुक्त किया गया है ।
.

[5]. सभी के लिए निर्देश :

भारतीय कम्पनियाँ व भारतीय नागरिक अपने निर्यात व् स्थानांतरण से अर्जित डॉलर असीमित दिनों के लिए भारतीय बैंकों में जमा कर सकेंगे।
विदेशी कंपनी अपने निर्यात व स्थानांतरण से अर्जित डॉलर भारतीय या विदेशी बैंक में असीमित दिनों तक रख सकेगी।
भारतीय नागरिक रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया की अनुमति के बिना विदेशी बैंकों में डॉलर नहीं रख सकेंगे हालाँकि लौटे हुए अप्रवासी भारतीय विदेशी बैंको में दस वर्ष तक अपने खाते संचालित कर सकेंगे/ रख सकेंगे।
.

[6]. भारतीय नागरिकों के लिए निर्देश :

रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया व भारतीय बैंक रूपए के बदले डॉलर नहीं देंगे हालाँकि भारत सरकार के आयात की स्थिति में ऐसा किया जा सकेगा।
यदि निजी नागरिक आयात करना चाहे तो उन भारतीय या विदेशी कम्पनियों, जिनके पास भारतीय या विदेशी बैंकों में डॉलर है, से डॉलर प्राप्त करना होगा।
.

[7]. भारतीय नागरिकों के लिए निर्देश :

कोई भारतीय नागरिक अथवा कंपनी जिसने निर्यात अथवा स्थानांतरण के द्वारा डॉलर अर्जित किया है उन्हें रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया में जमा किये हुए/ बेचे हुए डॉलर की मात्रा के अनुपात में 30 % तक आयकर में छूट मिलेगी।
.

[8]. आयकर की गणना में आयात को कटौती योग्य खर्चो में शामिल नहीं किया जाएगा ।
.

[9]. कस्टम बोर्ड चेयरमेन के लिए निर्देश:

हीरे के आयात को किम्बरली प्रक्रिया, प्रमाणन योजना (KPCS) या किसी अन्य प्रमाण पत्र की आवश्यकता नहीं होगी।
.

[10]. भारतीय नागरिकों के लिए निर्देश:

कोई भी कंपनी जो सोना या चाँदी आयात करती है वह आयातित सोने या चांदी की मात्रा के अनुपात में सोना आयातित अंक अथवा चाँदी आयातित अंक अर्जित करेगी। अंक हस्तांतरणीय होंगे।
अंकों के खातों की रिजर्व बैंक ऑफ़ इंडिया द्वारा नामित बैंकों द्वारा देखरेख/ निगरानी की जाएगी ।
.

[11]. भारतीय नागरिको के लिए निर्देश :

कोई भारतीय नागरिक जो कि स्वर्ण या चांदी या मिश्रित गहनों का निर्यात करता है, को उस सीमा तक स्वर्ण या चांदी के निर्यात की अनुमति होगी, जिस सीमा तक उसने स्वर्ण या चांदी के आयात अंक अर्जित किये है या आयात अंको का स्थानान्तरण/हस्तांतरण किया है ।
.

[12]. सभी के लिए निर्देश :

कोई भी भारतीय या विदेशी कंपनी स्वर्ण या चांदी या धातु या धात्विक अयस्क का निर्यात प्रधानमन्त्री कार्यालय या उनके द्वारा नियुक्त अधिकारी की अनुमति बिना नहीं कर सकेगी ।
.

[13]. कस्टम बोर्ड चेयरमेन के लिए निर्देश :

यदि किसी समाप्य सामग्री का निर्यात किया जाता है, जिसमे कि स्वर्ण या चांदी सम्मिलित है, तो ऐसे स्वर्ण या चांदी की कुल कीमत निर्यातित सामग्री के 1% मूल्य से अधिक की नही हो सकेगी ।
अन्य सभी धातुओ के लिए निर्यात शुल्क निर्यातित सामग्री के 30% के बराबर होगा ।
.

[14]. सभी के लिए निर्देश :

यदि निर्यातक द्वारा रिज़र्व बेंक ऑफ़ इंडिया को डॉलर बेचे गए है, तब बेचे गए डॉलर की कीमत के 30% की सीमा तक उन्हें आय कर में छूट प्राप्त होगी, अन्य सभी परिस्थितियों में निर्यात द्वारा अर्जित आय पूरी तरह से कर योग्य होगी ।
.

[15]. भारतीय नागरिको के लिए निर्देश :

ऐसी भारतीय कंपनी जो कि विदेशी मुद्रा रखती है, को रखी गयी विदेशी मुद्रा पर महीने की आखिरी तारीख को 0.1% की दर से संपत्ति कर का भुगतान करना होगा ।
.

[16]. भारतीय नागरिको के लिए निर्देश :

कोई भी भारतीय कम्पनी जो कि विदेशी मुद्रा रखती है, को रखी गयी विदेशी मुद्रा पर अर्जित किये गए लाभ पर महीने की आखिरी तारीख को 30% पूंजीगत कर का भुगतान करना होगा, जबकि पूंजीगत हानि को आगामी 36 माह तक अग्रेषित किया जा सकेगा ।

_________________

ड्राफ्ट का अंत

How China \ USA used their Military might to extract bargain from India? Solution : print law-drafts that will reduce inefficiency of LOCAL (not FDI) factory owners so that manufacturing and weapon manufacturing improves. And dont waste time with SoMoKe-andhbhagats as they oppose this law-drafts
.
China had sent troops into Ladakh and China went out only after Indian cancelled anti-dumping duty on chinese made imported solar panels !! IOW, using its Military might , China forced India to change the policies.
.
Now USA didnt send any troops or didnt even fire a bullet. Then how did USA used its Militart might to force India to accept nuclear deal and other deals like GST?
.
USA in 1998 had paid Pakistan and provided logistic help to do Kargil on India. And only after India agreed to allow US media and US insurance companies to do business in India, and stop nuclear tests, did USA allowed France to give technology to use laser guided bombs to India !! Without LGB, we would have lost Kargil war !!! I repear, if France had not given technology to install LGB , then we would have lost Kargil war !! IOW, USA via Pakistan's Military, forced India to change economic as well as military policies !!!
.
Come 2015 , and things remain same as they change more. Even today, USA uses threat to provide Military support to Pakistan and enable Pakistan to send 100s of Kasabs into India and destabilize the PM. And using this threat of overt attack via Pakistan, USA has been successfully able to force the nuclear deal on us.
.
Solution?
.
1. Stop wasting time with SoMoKe-andhbhagats and CoBhASwaRa-workers. WHY? see-2
.
2. Please publicize law-drafts that can reduce the inefficiencies of LOCAL (loca india, not FDI) factory owners in India. Which law-drafts? You might want to ask SoMoKe-andbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers and see what law-drafts they give.The law-drafts they give will convince you that they are all waste of time.
.
3. The law-drafts I propose are TCP as in see section-1.3 of rahulmehta.com/301.htm , RTR as in see chap-6, DDMRCAM chap-5, wealth tax chap-25, streamline income tax, remove sez, remove vat \ gst \ octroi \ excise etc , Jury System chap-21 etc etc.
.
Please order your MP via SMS to print these law-drafts in Gazette. For SMS text, please see fb.com/notes/10151308223491922

How Mauritius Route benefits USUK economies at the cost of India economy?
.
SOLUTION : stop wasting time with SoMoKe-andhbhagats , CoBhApSwaRa-workers and please order your MP via SMS to end Mauritius route.
.
(1) Consider company-A which is Indian company and invests in USUK . Then for whatever profit, it makes in USUK, it will pay 40% income tax in USUK and then 0% in India.
.
(2) Same way, consider company-B which is American company , and invests in India, Then it will pay 30% to 40% income tax in India and then 10% to 0% in USUK. Now many US companies in India have obtained income tax exemption , and so in such case they will pay 0% tax in India and 40% in USUK.
.
(3) But consider company-C of USUK. Say it invests in India via Mauritius. Then in India, that company will pay 0% tax !! And in Mauritius it will pay only 1% tax , and then in USA it will pay 39% tax !!!
.
Why cant Indian company-A in example-1 do same? well, becaue USUK has NO such tax exemption treaty with any country !!! All companies in USUK have to pay tax in USUK.
.
Then why does USUK sign contract with India that "US companies in India will pay tax in USUK and not in India, while Indian companies in USUK will pay taxes in USUK and not India?" Why does USA use Mauritius route? That if open explicit agreement is made, then citizens of India will come to know that India is openly dominated by USUK. And that will lower the reputation of SoMoKe and their ability to rule Indians. So a cover of Mauritius treaty is used to hide this open domination in tax laws !!
.
SOLUTION :-
.
1. Stop wasting time with SoMoKe-andhbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers . BST-workers too are now OPENLY supporting Mauritius route. proof : ask them if they have sent order to MP via SMS to cancel Mauritius route. None of them did despite. They only fool you by putting picture of Rajiv Dixitjee in their profiles.
.
2. Please order YOU MP via SMS to cancel Mauritius treaty. . You can send following order to MP via SMS "dear MP, I order you to cancel tax treaty with Mauritius . #CancelMauritiusTreaty ".
.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Mon Feb 09 2015 16:55:52 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

चुनावी नतीजे कुछ भी हो, मोदी साहेब की कलई खुल चुकी है कि, वो कंट्रोल कर रहे है ।
.

सोशल मिडिया पर चिचियाहट करने वालो से यह तथ्य छुपा हुआ नहीं है, कि मिडिया बिकाऊ है, भांड है, दलाल है और भी सब कुछ है, पर निष्पक्ष तो कम से कम कतई नहीं है ।
.

वास्तविकता यह है कि मिडिया पेड है, पेड था तथा तब तक पेड ही रहेगा जब तक कि मिडिया को प्रजा अधीन नहीं किया जाता ।

पेड मिडिया की समस्या के समाधान का प्रस्तावित ड्राफ्ट यहाँ देखे :

tinyurl.com/RtrDdAdhyaksh
.

UPA शासन के दौरान मोदिभक्त इस भ्रम में पड़े रहे कि चूंकि कोंग्रेस सत्ता में है, इसलिए सोनिया, राहुल वगेरह मिडिया को कंट्रोल करते है, तो दिल्ली चुनाव का चाबुक उनकी तन्द्रा भंग करने के पर्याप्त होना चाहिए कि न तो तब सोनिया और न अब मोदी मिडिया को कंट्रोल करते है ।
.

पेड मिडिया को तब भी MNCs/मिशनरीज़ ही कंट्रोल करती थी और अब भी वे ही कंट्रोल करती है ।
.

यदि मोदी साहेब मिडिया को कंट्रोल करते तो मिडिया केजरीवाल को मोदी के बरअक्स इतनी ताकत के साथ खड़ा करने का दुस्साहस नही करता ।
.

यह MNCs ही है जो पेड मिडिया के माध्यम से पीएम को कंट्रोल करती है, जबकि मोदिभक्त इस पिनक में है कि 283 सांसद अपनी जेब में रखने वाले मोदी साहेब कंट्रोल कर रहे है ।
.

यह MNCs के ही बस कि बात है कि करोड़ो अंधभक्त, आरएसएस प्रचारक, बीएसटी के कार्यकर्ता तथा नेताओं की फ़ौज होने के बावजूद MNCs ने केजरीवाल नामक सांप को 5 साल तक मोदी साहेब के गले में लटकाने के करीब करीब पहुंचा ही दिया है ।

*श्री युत केजरीवाल के लिए सर्प विशेषण का प्रयोग मोदी भक्तो की भावनाओं के अनुरूप किया गया है ।
.

यदि MNCs केजरीवाल को CM बनाने में कामयाब हो जाती है, 14 साल में एक भी चुनाव न हारने वाली मोदी-शाह की जोड़ी का आभामंडल धूमिल हो जाएगा, और मोदी भक्तो के चेहरे 2-4 दिन तक बिना थप्पड़ के भी लाल दिखाई देंगे ।
.

यदि आप चाहते है कि भारत के नेता चुनाव जीतने के लिए MNCs और पेड मिडिया पर निर्भर नहीं रहे, तो निष्पक्ष मिडिया की स्थापना के लिए दूरदर्शन अध्यक्ष को प्रजा अधीन करने के लिए अपने सांसद को यह कानूनी ड्राफ्ट SMS द्वारा भेज कर इसे गेजेट में छापने का आदेश करे ।

tinyurl.com/RtrDdAdhyaksh

Why RSS-BJP-VHP-workers and other Hinduvaadies and should leave RSS-BJP-VHP-apex and work with Right to recall Group, even though many recallists do NOT support the concept of Hindu Rashtra.
.
Our group MSSS = Mahatma Sachinadranath Sanyal Sangathan is registered. We at RRG are yet to register our political party. But thats a minor issue. The key things in our agenda are law-drafts.
.
IMO, the Hinduvaadies across India should leave RSS-VHP-BJP-apex and instead work on law-drafts proposed by us at Right to Recall Group. You may work with RRG or any other organization. That IMO isnt important. They key thing I request you is that you should dump time-wasting vote-catching programs of RSS-BJP-VHP-apex and focus on RRG-law-drafts.
.
WHY ?
.
RSS-BJP-VHP-workers are committed. But the RSS-BJP-VHP-apex leaders are just psudeo-Hinduvaadi. They have no commitment for any Hinduvaadi causes. They simply show Hinduvaadi posture to attract you Hinduvaadies to ensure that you dont create any political alternatives and thus monopoly of BJP continues.
.
eg. For BJP-apex-leaders , "gaay hamari maataa he , isase vote aataa he" i.e. for leaders of BJP-RSS-VHP, "cow is out mother and cow gets us votes" and thats all. Cow-protection is just a "chunavi jumala" i.e. "election gimmick" for them.
.
eg2 Same for other causes like freeing temples from Govt control. Are temples in BJP states free from Govt control? NO. Congress used the temples to enrich congress leaders and now BJP leaders are using temples to enrich themselves !!!
.
So how can we at Right to Recall Group and our drafts can help?
.
We recallists at RRG agree on some key law-drafts such as TCP (see section-1.3 of http://rahulmehta.com/301.htm ) , RTR-PM, RTR-CM, DDMRCM , wealth tax etc. On many we differ amongst each other. But we all agree that differences will be resolved using TCP and voting.
.
Now which agenda items of Hinduvaad can benefit from our RRG proposals ?
.
1. stopping cow slaughter ----- we have proposed law-drafts needed to stop cow slaughter --- pls see https://fb.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152908801793103/
.
2. reducing jeev hatya ---- we have proposed law-drafts that will reduce jeev hatya , pls see above draft
.
3. population control ---- only we at Right to Recall group campaign for law-draft that punishes those who have 3 or more children with fines and imprisonment
.
4. giving tax benefits to those who have 2 child so that couples dont stop at just 1 child )
.
5. law-drafts to free govt control over temples ---- https://fb.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152606376338103/
.
6. law-drafts that will reduce conversion without any use of govt force on missionaries.
.
7. RJB , KJB and Kashi Vishvanath
.
8. law-drafts needed to reduce love jihad (see chap on love jihad in above link and my video on same topic ) . The law-draft says that "wife can chose which marriage code will decide their marriage"
.
IOW, there are 10s of Hinduvaai causes that we are sincerely working on.
.
And now let me also make it clear where things end.
.
DIFFERENCES BETWEEN MANY RECALLISTS AND HINDUVAADIES
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
.
At this point, I want to also make it clear where I and many of us do NOT support concept of Hindu Rasthra. I believe in true secular Rashtra . Many countries like USA , UK etc are secular, and truely secular, I have lived in USA for 8 years, and I can vouch that USG and US administration is highly secular to core. And I want to make India at least as secular as USA or more secular. I oppose psuedo-
.
However , not of all of us support the concept Hindu Rashtra. Many of us might be spporting, but not all of us. To be specific, I do NOT support concept of Hindu Rashtra.
.
And I also oppose use of force to stop Missionaries and use of force to stop conversions.
.
Also, while VHP etc claim that they support Hindu Rashtra , But till date, VHP-leaders never ever gave law-drafts to bring that Hindu Rashtra. It has always been pure hollow talk to ensure that Hinduvaadies dont form any political alternatives.
.
======
,
Also, Hinduvaadies are being misled by RSS-VHP-BJP-leaders. The biggest threat to India as Hinduism is form FDI, which each and every apex-leader of RSS-VHP-BJP supports FDI !! The FDI will enable MNC-owners to establish their feet in India, and via that, they are bound to promote Missionaries and also bribe IAS / IPS / judges to take actions to protect and promote them. And RSS-VHP-BJP-leaders dont even explain how FDI will lead to expansion of Missionaries and demise of temples !! Their silence on how "FDI will increase conversion" itself shows that VHP-BJP-RSS leaders are only psuedo-Hindivaadies. As far as FDI goes, all three --- SoMoKe --- Sonia, NaMo, ArKe -- one and the same, support FDI. And what is worse is ---- SoMoKe and RSS-VHP-BJP-leaders also support FDI in paidmedia !!!
.
=====
.
So all in all, even though many of us at Right to recall group DO NOT support concept of Hindu Rashtra, the law-drafts we have proposed will serve Hinduvaad more than useless talks of VHP-RSS-BJP-leaders. So I request all RSS-BJP-VHP-workers and Hinduvaadies to dump RSS-VHP-BJP-leaders and increase publicize the law-drafts like law-drafts to free temples from govt control, law-drafts to stop cow slaughter, law-drafts to reduce jeev hatya, law-drafts to reduce love jihad, law drafts to end art-370 etc.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Feb 10 2015 10:23:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How MNC_owners and Missionaries defeated nationalists / hinduvaadies / swadeshi / anti-corruption activists?
.
Because nationalists / hinduvaadies / swadeshi / anti-corruption activists all became andjbhagats of psuedo-nationalists , psuedo-hinduvasdi etc. leaders
.
SOLUTION : The nationalists etc shoudl campaign for REAL law-drafts and stop andhbhakti of their psuedo-nationalist , psuedo-hinduvaadies leaders
.
NaMo-andbhagats will still say --- see BJP kept its vote share intact. But well, in past 16 months, why didnt BJP increase its vote share even by 5%? If Congress voters left Congress, why did they chose AAP and not BJP? And no --- not all of them are Muslims. In fact MAJORITY of Hindus in Delhi voted for AAP. And worse, a large number of anti-AAP , anti-Congress voters didnt vote as they see BJP equally bad optiobn. And thats why, voting % in feb-2014 was LESS than may-2014 despite the fact that may-2014 was summer, and voting % in summer is always about 4% less than good weather days.
.
To me, question why BJP leaders lost is useless question. The question is --- despite being openly anti-national, anti-hindu and psuedo-anti-corruption, and despite best efforts of nationalist / hinduvaadi / wedeshi activists, how come ArKe could get so many votes? i.e. real question to me is --- why are nationalist activists losing against stooges of MNC-owners and Missionaries?
.
My answer is ---
.
(a) Corruption of policemen , judges , IAS etc is a real issue. And BJP-leaders are pro-corruption. So these leaders did NOTHING to reduce corruption.
.
(b) nationalist activists decided to confine to andhbhakti and refused to promote REAL anti-corruption-law-drafts. So corruption continued.
.
(c) And so paidmedia could project psuedo-anti-corruption hero as REAL anti-corruption-hero and collect all votes.
.
One can blame MNC-paid paidmedia on and on and on. But if in 250 days, nationalists had worked to promote REAL anti-corruption-law-drafts, then corruption would have become US level or lower within 60 days. But instead they all decided to confine to slogan shouting, cap wearing, broom weilding, selfie uploading, rallying and running door to door on election day. And the latest fad was --- wearing expensive suits and trousers with golden verticle stripes having name of their God emrodaried (similar to many saints wearing cloth with "ram naam" written all over) !!! Obviously, this hard work doesnt reduce corruption by 1%.
.
ArKe is 100% psuedo-anti-corruption. But the fact that BJP-leaders are all pro-corruption enabled MNC-owners to project this psuedo-solution as real solution !!
.
And to make matter worse, nationalist activists support BJP-leaders decision to promote FDI in everything, including FDI in paidmedia !!!
.
Well, folks, now unless REAL anti-corruption-law-drafts comes, the whole country is in the hands of MNC-paid paidmedia. With MNC-paid paidmedia , the MNC-owners can swing votes in whichever direction they want and blackmail Congress, BJP as well as AAP leaders to fullest. If AAP leaders dont follow MNC-owners and Missionaries agenda, then in coming elections, they can create anti-AAP-wave and make BJP or Congress win. If BJP-leaders dont follow MNC-owners' and Missionaries' agends, then using paidmedia, they can make AAP win every coming election.
.
All in all, due to "slogan-shouting and do nothing else" approach of nationalists activists, all leaders in India will now compete to serve interests of MNC-owners and Missionaries and do nothing else.
.
SOLUTION ?
.
Solution I propose is that nationalist, activists should stop wearing fancy uniforms , stop morning walks, stop slogan shouting, stop brooming streets , stop uploading selfies showing them brooming streets, stop wearing caps, stop wearing masks and STOP WORSHIIPING THEIR TALL LEADERS. They should also stop wearing expensive suits and toursers with tellow vertical stripes with names of their leaders in it.
.
The nationalists, hinduvaadies , anti-corruption and swadeshi should read and spread information on law-drafts that can reduce corruption, reduce inefficiencies of INDIAN non-FDI factory owners , reduce inefficiencies of INDIAN non-FDI weapon manufacturers , reduce poverty , free temples from govt control, end cow slaughter, impose 2 child restriction etc
.
Which law-drafts? The law-draftsI have proposed are in rahulmehta.com/301.htm . And in notes in my profile. You are welcome to ask YOUR leaders to provide law-drafts.

.
Due to exceptionally corrupt leaders that Congress / BJP had , we have solved problems that west solved in 1950 !! Since 1950s, almost every town in USA gets 24 hour running water !!! Even in winters, when water freezes, the pipelines are heated to give supply of running waters. And many cities implemented in 1920s. Thats because a simple rule --- every connection in city is metered !!! So there is low wastage, and there is always money to create supply.
.
Is water meter high tech? NO. They have been around since 1800 AD !!!
.
IOW, by making every water connection metered, everyone in India could have obtained 24 hour running water in 1960s. And we still dont have that.
.
Now consider a US company named Haliburton. This company is a marvel in engineering. It is capable of putting water lines. Not just water lines, but it is capable of converting 100 sqkm of wasteland into a FULLY functional city within 6 months flat !!! And that city will have houses, offices, restaurants, electricity supply, roads, water lines, sewer, bridges where needed, tunnels where needed, bus system, theaters and everything !!! And all that in 6 months. After winning gulf war, when USA started looting oil of Kuwait and Iraq, this company had built six citizens each housing 2 lakh to 5 lakh people in Kuwait !!! And all that in 6 months !!.
.
So if every connection is metered, including sub-connections, then within 3 months, Haliburton can provide 24 hour running water to everyone in Delhi.
.
And free wifi of upto 1 GB per person 64 kpbs is child's play for Haliburton.
.

.

YogYad is openly pro-Missionaries. ArKe also supports FDI in everything including railways, defence, ports and mining. All in all, AAP can be cause royal disaster in India.
.

.

Yes, the key thing is --- without FDI. And AFAIK, that is possible only using law-drafts like Jury System , DDMRCM, TCP, Right to recall police chief, RTR judges, wealth tax, abolishing excise \ vat \ gst \ octroi etc . These law-drafts are in http://rahulmehta.com/301.htm in section-1.3 , chap-5 , section-6.6, chap-25 etc
.
So IMO, activists should focus on law-drafts and give up neta-andhbhakti. .

Instead of names of parties and candidates, its time we write name of TV-channels on ballot papers !!

.
MNC-owners and Missionaries have used and now thrown away Congress ; for the time being. And they have a new Congress, namely AAP. AAP is "better" in one way because AAP-apex doesnt touch bribes and black money. I am serious --- no sarcasm. No AAP leader will ever take any bribe and will stay away from cash money and confine to cheques.
.
These AAP-leaders will serve MNC-masters and Missionary-masters without taking a penny. And so readers will send them donations needed to run offices, and a large number of volunteers will also provide free service.
.

.

All MNC-owners need to do is pay the paidmediamen to run a massive campaign against corruption, exposing BOTH, leaders of BJP as well as Congress. And both will lose reputation. Congress will be ordere dto transfer all its Muslim \ Dalit votes to AAP. BJP will be confined to its 20% to 30% core voters. And all middle class anti-corruption voters will flock to this new angel of anti-corruption --- namely AAP. And so BJP will get 4% seats, no more.
.

.
IOW, it is time, nationalist , hinduvaadies, swadeshi realize that they simply dont have option of putting a pro-corruption party like BJP before voters. If the nationalists , hinduvaadi, swadeshi etc put pro-corruption leaders like BJP-leaders before voters, then MNC-owners using paidmedia will easily convince voters to vote for AAP-leaders as AAP-leaders never take bribes and never touch cash. And while paidmedia is corrupt from top to bottom, there is no paper proof that paidmediamen are paid by MNC-owners and Missionaries. And there is no paper trail that AAP-leaders are agents are MNC-owners and Missionaries. So if contest is between BJP-leaders and AAP-leaders, as Delhi-elections proved, it is paidmedia which will decide the winner !!!
.
What is worse is that MNC_owners will NOT defeat BJP-leaders but simply blackmail them to get more and more FDI in return for "delayed defeat". The deal could be as follows :
.
1. Ahmedabad Municipal Corporation election are coming in oct-2015
.
2. Over 80% Congress \ BJP Corporators are corrupt !!! They are assorted liquor mafia, land mafia, education mafia and some are high interest mafia who charge 20% interest per month from poor !!!
.
3. If MNC-owners via assorted NGOs give assistance to AAP Ahmedabad unit, then AAP can field 192 honest candidates.
.
4. The MNC-paid paidmediamen can make corruption and mafiagiri of corporators an issue.
.
5. Congress can be ordered to transfer all its votes to AAP.
.
6. in oct-2010 elections , some 55% voters had NOT voted !! And of the 45% who voted, some 60% voted for BJP and 40% voted for Congress.
.
7. If paidmedia in Ahmedabad supports AAP the way paidmedia supported AAP in Delhi, then Ahmedabad BJP till will be get no more than 9/192 seats !! And rest all will go to AAP !!!
.
This may look absurd, but look at what happened in dec-2013, may-2014 and feb-2015 in delhi. In dec-2013, BJP got 33/70 seats. In LS-may-2014 , BJP had majority in 60/70 assembly segments. And the numbers of 33/70 and 60/70 both came down to 3/70 !!!
.
So what is worse is that MNC-owners and Missionaries and THREATEN BJP-leaders to promote FDI, in return of keeping low. IOW , BJP-leaders will win elections, but sector by sector of economy will pass into the hands , and soul after soul will pass in the hands of Missionaries. The BJP-leaders will show FDI-led growth as their hard work and gloat. And they may attain some easy goals like go-raksha . But once MNC-owners takeover economy, in next 30 years , their agents Missionaries will wipe out much of Hinduism, and once Hinduism is gone, all cows will land in slaughter house anyway.
.
Solution I propose has 3 parts.
.
First, the nationalists , swadeshi, hinduvaadies should request voters to send orders to MPs via SMS , and the SMS should have URL or URI to the law-drafts that will make links between paidmedia and MNC-owners open. The law-draft I propose is Right to Recall Doordarshan Chairman (which incidently, all Congress / BJP / AAP leaders have opposed !!). This law-draft will enable citizens to expel journalists and Chairman of Doordarshan who are hiding the information, and this journalists and Chairman will deter from hiding information.
.
Second, nationalists , swadeshi, hinduvaadies etc should request voters to send orders to MPs via SMS , and the SMS should have URL or URI to the law-drafts to release temples from govt control , end gohatya, reduce jeev hatya, reduce poverty, reduce land prices, reduce black money in Indian land deals, cancel vat / gst / excise / octroi / lbt etc , implement wealth tax, oppose inheritance tax NaMo / JayantSinha are trying to bring , TCP i.e. enable voters to put their coomplaints on PM's website , right to recall over police chief / judges etc , JurySys etc. Please see the text of SMS in https://www.facebook.com/notes/10151308223491922 .
.
And lastly, activists should stop support corrupt leader and or pro-FDI leader NO matter how much nationalist, hinduvaad , gorakshavaad etc they support. All leaders, such as Mihan Bhagwat , who shun the topic on corruption / FDI by citing useless arguments like "our job is to build national character, and not oppose corruption or FDI" should be shunned. Or someone like Togadia, who says "our job is to protect Hinduism, not oppose FDI or oppose corruption !!". A pro-corruption leader will NOT survive against AAP and will surrender, and then will become pro-FDI leader. And once FDI takes over whole economy, it is matter of few years that Maths \ Science education will collapse, local religions will get wiped out and India will become a giant phillipines or get divided into 10-15 phillipines.

.

.

.
In this 2-party contest , AAP got 53% votes and BJP got 33%. And amongst educated, more educated voted for AAP then BJP (all surveys, no hard proof). Its true that amongst less educated and illiterate, more voted for AAP than BJP (again surveys).
.
Caste this time played role, but secondary role. And both AAP / BJP had used caste composition in their candidates. Regionalism played some role, but again, secondary. And both parties gave tickets to candidates of all regions. And muslim voters are only 12%.
.
So we have 2 party contest, a platform where castism / regionalism played secondary role, and most voters were educated.
.
Yey, BJP lost. That too , by 53/33 , and even if all muslim voters are exclused, BJP lost by (53-12)/33 = 41/33 !!
.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Feb 17 2015 03:59:01 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Following are the problems with some 90% to 95% judges , that are absent in over 99% Jurors, and why over 90% of judge smisuse their discretionary powers ---
.
1. Longlasting nexuses with lawyers and powerful criminals or powerful asils ---- The Jurors are large number and change in with every case. And one Jury gets only one case. And over 99% cases get resolved in 3-5 days in Jury System. And so lawyers, powerful criminals and powerful asils have less chance to make nexuses. And the nexuses if and once made, arent useful in next case as next case has different Jury, So over 80% to 95% judges across India / world have nexuses with relative lawyers, lawyers, powerful criminals and otherwise wealthy clients. Jurors dont have such nexuses.
.
2. corruption due to nexuses ---- stronger the nexuses, higher the corruption.
.
3. Nepotism --- Over 80% judges have tendencies to favor their relative lawyers. Since Jurors are decided at random from a population of lakhs, chances that a Jurors will be relative of lawyer ate next to zero. So nepotism is structurally impossible in Jury System
.
4. Incitement = lalach of promotion ---- over 95% judges obbey wrong judgments and order of senior judges because they want promotions. They are also eager to satify legal or even illegal demands of relative lawyers of senior judges to get promotions. Jurors have no system of promotion. So no Jurir has incitement = lalach of promotions
.
5. Fear of transfer , suspension, expulsion ---- over 90% judges obey worng written or oral orders of senior judges because they are afraid that senior judges will transfer , suspend of expel them !! Whereas Jurors cant be transferred !! And worst case is expulsion from trial, but that doesnt effect his salary or career of standing.
.
IOW , Juror may or may not gain or lose from misuse of discretionary powers. But a judge has EVERY chance of gaining by misusing his discretionary powers .
.
The worst damage due to judge system comes --- uphelding "unfair" laws whereas biggest benefit of Jury System is that -- Jurors pay no regard to "unfair" laws and OPENLY give judgments against "unfair" law. eg in USA , the judges kept giving rulings in favor of slavery, while Jurors in northern states kept acquitting slaves, and finally that forced law-makers of USA to abolish slavery !!
.
And many times, a "bad" law goes on and on, as society knows no alternative law which is less bad. But when a new alternatiev farme word for less bad law comes, but it is yet to be enacted, the difference between Jury System and judge system becomes more crucial. The judges for their career interests, keep upholding the "bad" law as long as elitemen keep benefitting from it, While Jurors start giving judgments against prevailing "bad" law, which forces law-makers to cancel the prevailing law and adopt the "less bad" law. IOW, change of ;laws is faster in Jury System than in judgment.
.
A nation which rapidly junks "bad" laws and adopts "less bad" laws will move faster, and make better gizmos / weapons. And then it will enslave the nation which are changing their laws at lesser speed. That one reason why countries with Jury System have been able to defeat / enslave countries with judge system. .
.
The Jury System of USA has weakened. But it is still there and very strong. While India has no Jury System. So courts in India are far far more corrupt, nepotic, nexused and lower judges have lost moral compass and give judgments as if they are slaves of higher judges. All wrong laws which benefit elitemen are upheld by magistrates as well as supreme judges. So strength ratio of India / USA is worsening day by day. And each arm of USA --- Military , factories, Missionaries etc are becoming stronger and stronger compared to corresponding area in India i.e. Indian Military , factories, temples etc. It is matter of time, USA's Military literally devors Indians Military, US companies devor Indian companies, and US's missionaries devor India's temples/religions etc. Unless, we also make our courts at least as "less corrupt" and "less nepotic" as US courts. And solution I propose are --- right to recall judges, right to recall public prosecutors and Jury System in every court from Magistrate's court to Supreme Court. The drafts for these laws are in in https://www.facebook.com/notes/10150422423416922 .
.
In next status, I will explain why SoMoKe oppose Jury System and RTR-judges, and why paidmedia-owners will give credit to SoMoKe in case SoMoKe/paidmedia-owners fail to stop RTR/Jury from coming into India.

We generally give credit of brnging freedom to India to congress leaders like Duratma Gandhi, Jawaher Ghazi etc. Whereas , reality is that till last moment they they supported British !!! And freedom came ONLY due to efforts of Rashtrapita Mahatma Subhash Bose and others like Mahatma Bhagat Singh etc. and paid-historians gave minimal credit to them !! The damage was not just that wrong people got credit and right people didnt. The damage was that --- importance of weapons got defocused !!! If Rashtrapita Mahatma Bose had got credit for freedom, then whole population would have understood the importance of weapons in bringing justice and freedom. But since Duratma Gandhi , Jawaherlal Ghazi etc got credit for bringing freedom, the population never came to know about importance of weapons in bringing justice and freedom !!
.
Same way, RTR / Jury may never come in India, in which case, India is all set to become another giant phillipnes. Dear Citizens of India,
.
The real problem with Mauritius \ Singapore \ Fiji routes is as follows
.
1. When an Indian company \ person invests in USA \ UK \ Japan \ China, directly or via any country, it pays income tax in USA \ UK \ Japan \ China and not in India .
.
2. When a USA \ UK entity invests in India , via Mauritius , it pays tax in USA \ UK and not in India !!! And when a Japanese company invests in India via Singapore , it will pay taxes in Japan and NOT in India !!! And when a Chinese company invests in India via Fiji, it will pay taxes in China and NOT in India !!!
.
DO YOU SUPPORT \ OPPOSE THESE TAX TREATIES ?
.

.

.

The real issue Mauritius route which is problem as follows
.
1. When an Indian entity invests in USA \ UK \ Japan \ China, directly or via any country, it pays income tax in USA and not in India .

.
.
Example :
.
1. Say Indian invests $1000 million in USA directly or via any company. He he makes profit of $100 million. Then he has to pay taxes of $40 million in USA
.
2. Say American invests $1000 million in India via Mauritius. Say he makes profit of $100 million. Then he has to pay taxes of $0 in India, $2 million in Mauritius and $38 million in USA !!!
.
IOW, US Govt gets taxes from income generated in India !!
.
These treaty means "Gora \ Japata \ Chapata will pay no tax in India but pay tax only in USUK \ Japan \ China , while desi will pay all taxes in India as well as USUS \ Japan\China ".

Wed Feb 18 2015 03:13:49 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

On facebook, pls divide all your fbfriends into LISTS , and when you put status posts, please select which FRIEND LIST should see that specific status post.
.
Or else, all your fbfriends will see all your posts and so your fbfriends will simply unfollow you to keep their newsfeed relevent and clean, and save their time.
.
So when you make important post which you want ALL to see, that post will have less audience.
.
eg I saw a status post from one fbfriend of mine, who is NOT a close real life friend, " had a great breakfast at .... ". Now if that post was from my reallife friend, I would have liked-commented-ignored it. But since it was from a non-real-life fbfriend, I clicked "unfollow" that person , because I dont want my newsfeed to get cluttered from non-political non-serious posts from non-real-life fbfriend !! So next time, when that person posts a serious political post, fb will NOT show it to me. It is my loss, and also his loss.
.
Instead, if that person had made friend lists such as "real life close friends" , "real life not close friends" , "political colleagues" etc etc and shown that "had great breakfast" status ONLY to "real life close friends" , then I would not have seen that status, and I would NOT have unfollowed him !!
.
So please classify your fbfriends into friend lists , and please dont make all non-serious posts public . Or else, more and more will only unfollow you.

If AAP got votes for freebies, then why didnt BJP start an AAP-spoiler which promises twice of what AAP promised and cut away all its votes?
.
BJP-supporters and NaMo-andhbhagats want to count leaves and miss the forest.
.
They go on and on and on and on counting promises AAP made and dont answer a simple question --- say one had started a party called "Baap of AAP" i.e. BAAP. And say one had promises twice of what AAP promised. Than had that party won? Or had that party divided votes enabling BJP to win? If so, then why didnt BJP / NaMo / AmSh create a BAAP that would work as AAP-spoiler aka "AAP vote katua" enabling BJP to win?
.
Anyone can make promises twice of AAP.
.
eg

1. Will AAP give you 700 liters per day free water? Well, BAAP will give you 1000 liters of water free per day, and also 2 liters of milk free per day !!
.
2. Will AAP give you a house for your slum for free? Well, BAAP will give you TWO houses and one car free for your slum !!
.
3. Will AAP give you 10 colleges? Well, BAAP will give you 20 colleges and each will get BA \ BCom degree free sitting at home !!!
.
and so forth.
.
And then create a new party called "Grand Father of AAP" aka GFAAAP which promises four times.
.
eg
.
1. Will AAP give you 700 liters per day free water? Well, GFAAP will give you 2800 liters of water free per day, make a free swimming pool at your hime and and also 2 liters of milk free per day !!
.
2. Will AAP give you a house for your slum for free? Well, GFAP will give you 4 houses , with furnitures, and two cars free for your slum !!
.
3. Will AAP give you 10 colleges? Well, GFAAP will give you 20 colleges PLUS 5 IITs , and each will get a B Tech degree from IIT , free sitting at home !!!
.
====
.
So now BAAP \ GFAAP can cut AAP votes and enable BJP to win !!
=====
.
IOW, if promises were to play so much role, then defeating AAP was as easy as 1-2-3- for BJP. All they needed for to create a BAAP which promises twice of BAAP, create "Grand Father of AAP" aka GFAAAP which promises four times and so on. And these spoilers could have cut AAP votes enabling BJP to win.
.
=============
.
THE REAL FACTOR ----- The paidmedia
---------------------------------------------------
.
Promises are useless unless one has faith in person who makes promise. And that faith comes from real work or design or paidmedia . AAP had no real work or design. But AAP had paidmedia, paid by MNC-owners and Missionaries. And thats what created faith in ArKe.
.
The paidmedia projected ArKe as sincere and committed person, and that made his promises look real !! If BAAP or GFAAP comes, and makes twice and four times the promises, but have no paidmedia coverage, then they will all look as jokes, and so get zero votes.
.
IOW, the critical element is NOT the promises, but critical element is paidmedia.
,
By counting freebie promises AAP made, you are only counting leaves and ignoring the forest.
.
==============
.

.
The solution to paidmedia problem I suggest is Right to recall Doordarshan Chairman.
